 5134: 
 5135: # Full domain intelligence gathering
 5136: #
 5137: # _analyse_domain( $domain )
 5138: #
 5139: #   Runs the complete intelligence pipeline for a single domain name:
 5140: #   resolves its web-hosting IP (A record), mail-hosting IP (MX record),
 5141: #   DNS-hosting IP (NS record), and queries WHOIS for registrar, creation
 5142: #   date, expiry date, and abuse contact.  Each IP is enriched with
 5143: #   organisation name and abuse address via RDAP or WHOIS.
 5144: #
 5145: #   The result is cached in $self->{_domain_info}{$domain} so that if
 5146: #   the same domain is encountered in multiple sources (e.g. From: header
 5147: #   and body) the network queries are only performed once.
 5148: #
 5149: #   Called exclusively from _extract_and_analyse_domains(), which merges
 5150: #   the returned hashref with the { domain, source } record to produce
 5151: #   the per-domain hashrefs returned by mailto_domains().
 5152: #
 5153: # Entry criteria:
 5154: #   $domain  -- a defined, non-empty, lower-cased domain string with no
 5155: #               trailing dot (normalisation is the caller's responsibility).
 5156: #               Must not be in %TRUSTED_DOMAINS (filtering is the caller's
 5157: #               responsibility).
 5158: #   $self->{timeout}  -- used for all DNS and WHOIS socket operations.
 5159: #   $self->{_domain_info}  -- hashref cache; may already contain an entry
 5160: #                             for $domain from a prior call.
 5161: #
 5162: # Exit status:
 5163: #   Always returns a hashref reference -- never undef, never dies.
 5164: #   The hashref may be empty ({}) if all lookups fail or return nothing.
 5165: #   Keys present depend entirely on what the lookups return; all are
 5166: #   optional.  Possible keys and their types:
 5167: #     web_ip              string  -- dotted-quad IPv4 of A record
 5168: #     web_org             string  -- organisation owning web_ip
 5169: #     web_abuse           string  -- abuse contact for web_ip network
 5170: #     mx_host             string  -- lowest-preference MX hostname
 5171: #     mx_ip               string  -- dotted-quad IPv4 of mx_host
 5172: #     mx_org              string  -- organisation owning mx_ip
 5173: #     mx_abuse            string  -- abuse contact for mx_ip network
 5174: #     ns_host             string  -- first NS hostname returned
 5175: #     ns_ip               string  -- dotted-quad IPv4 of ns_host
 5176: #     ns_org              string  -- organisation owning ns_ip
 5177: #     ns_abuse            string  -- abuse contact for ns_ip network
 5178: #     registrar           string  -- registrar name from WHOIS
 5179: #     registrar_abuse     string  -- registrar abuse email from WHOIS
 5180: #     registered          string  -- creation date YYYY-MM-DD
 5181: #     expires             string  -- expiry date YYYY-MM-DD
 5182: #     recently_registered integer -- 1 if registered < 180 days ago
 5183: #     whois_raw           string  -- first 2048 bytes of WHOIS response
 5184: #
 5185: # Side effects:
 5186: #   Network I/O, subject to $self->{timeout}, in this order:
 5187: #     1. One A record lookup (_resolve_host) for the domain itself.
 5188: #     2. If web_ip found: one RDAP/WHOIS query (_whois_ip) -- up to two
 5189: #        TCP connections (IANA referral + authoritative registry).
 5190: #     3. If Net::DNS available: one MX record lookup.
 5191: #        If MX found: one A lookup for the MX hostname, then one
 5192: #        RDAP/WHOIS query for its IP (up to two TCP connections).
 5193: #     4. If Net::DNS available: one NS record lookup.
 5194: #        If NS found: one A lookup for the NS hostname, then one
 5195: #        RDAP/WHOIS query for its IP (up to two TCP connections).
 5196: #     5. Two TCP WHOIS connections for the domain itself: first to
 5197: #        whois.iana.org to obtain the TLD's authoritative registry,
 5198: #        then to that registry.
 5199: #   Worst-case total: 3 A lookups + 1 MX lookup + 1 NS lookup +
 5200: #   3x RDAP/WHOIS IP queries (up to 6 TCP connections) +
 5201: #   2 domain WHOIS TCP connections = up to 17 network operations.
 5202: #   Writes result hashref to $self->{_domain_info}{$domain} (cache).
 5203: #
 5204: # Notes:
 5205: #   Result is served from $self->{_domain_info}{$domain} on all
 5206: #   subsequent calls for the same domain within one parse_email() lifetime.
 5207: #   The cache is invalidated (reset to {}) by parse_email().
 5208: #
 5209: #   MX selection: the lowest-preference MX record is used (most preferred
 5210: #   server).  Only the primary MX is analysed; backup MXs are ignored.
 5211: #
 5212: #   NS selection: the first NS record returned by the resolver is used.
 5213: #   DNS resolvers do not guarantee a consistent ordering; the choice of
 5214: #   nameserver may vary between calls on different machines or at different
 5215: #   times.
 5216: #
 5217: #   MX and NS lookups are skipped entirely when Net::DNS is not installed.
 5218: #   In that case mx_*, ns_* keys are never present in the result.
 5219: #
 5220: #   WHOIS date fields (registered, expires) have time and timezone
 5221: #   components stripped by removing everything from the first 'T' or 'Z'
 5222: #   onward; the stored value is a plain YYYY-MM-DD string.  Parsing
 5223: #   uses the first matching pattern from a priority list; any fields not
 5224: #   found in the WHOIS response are absent from the result (not undef).
 5225: #
 5226: #   whois_raw is truncated to the first 2048 bytes of the raw WHOIS
 5227: #   response.  Structured fields (registrar, dates, abuse) are parsed from
 5228: #   the full response before truncation.
 5229: #
 5230: #   recently_registered is set to integer 1 when present; it is absent
 5231: #   (not 0) when the domain is not recently registered or when no creation
 5232: #   date was found.  The threshold is 180 days before time() at the moment
 5233: #   of analysis.
 5234: sub _analyse_domain {
●5235 → 5244 → 5251●5235 → 5244 → 0 5235: 	my ($self, $domain) = @_;
 5236: 
 5237: 	return $self->{_domain_info}{$domain} if $self->{_domain_info}{$domain};

 5725: 
 5726: =head1 AUTHOR
 5727: 
 5728: Nigel Horne, C<< <njh at nigelhorne.com> >>
 5729: 
 5730: =head1 ALGORITHM: DOMAIN INTELLIGENCE PIPELINE
 5731: 
 5732: For each unique non-infrastructure domain found in the email, the module
 5733: runs the following pipeline:
 5734: 
 5735:     Domain name
 5736:         |
 5737:         +-- A record  --> web hosting IP  --> RDAP --> org + abuse contact
 5738:         |
 5739:         +-- MX record --> mail server hostname --> A --> RDAP --> org + abuse
 5740:         |
 5741:         +-- NS record --> nameserver hostname  --> A --> RDAP --> org + abuse
 5742:         |
 5743:         +-- WHOIS (TLD whois server via IANA referral)
 5744:                +-- Registrar name + abuse contact
 5745:                +-- Creation date  (-> recently-registered flag if < 180 days)
 5746:                +-- Expiry date    (-> expires-soon or expired flags)
 5747: 
 5748: Domains are collected from:
 5749: 
 5750:     From:/Reply-To:/Sender:/Return-Path: headers
 5751:     DKIM-Signature: d=  (signing domain)
 5752:     List-Unsubscribe:   (ESP / bulk sender domain)
 5753:     Message-ID:         (often reveals real sending platform)
 5754:     mailto: links and bare addresses in the body
 5755: 
 5756: =head1 WHY WEB HOSTING != MAIL HOSTING != DNS HOSTING
 5757: 
 5758: A fraudster registering C<sminvestmentsupplychain.com> might:
 5759: 
 5760: =over 4
 5761: 
 5762: =item * Register the domain at GoDaddy (registrar)
 5763: 
 5764: =item * Point the NS records at Cloudflare (DNS/CDN)
 5765: 
 5766: =item * Have no web server at all (A record absent)
 5767: 
 5768: =item * Route the MX records to Google Workspace or similar
 5769: 
 5770: =back
 5771: 
 5772: Each of these parties has an abuse contact, and each can independently
 5773: take action to disrupt the spam/phishing operation.  The module reports
 5774: all of them separately.
 5775: 
 5776: =head1 RECENTLY-REGISTERED FLAG
 5777: 
 5778: Phishing domains are very commonly registered hours or days before the
 5779: spam run.  The module flags any domain whose WHOIS creation date is
 5780: less than 180 days ago with C<recently_registered =E<gt> 1>.
 5781: 
 5782: =head1 SEE ALSO
 5783: 
 5784: =over 4
 5785: 
 5786: =item * L<ARIN RDAP|https://rdap.arin.net/>
 5787: 
 5788: =item * L<Test Dashboard|https://nigelhorne.github.io/Email-Abuse-Investigator/coverage/>
 5789: 
 5790: =item * L<Net::DNS>, L<LWP::UserAgent>, L<HTML::LinkExtor>, L<MIME::QuotedPrint>,
 5791: 
 5792: =back
